# 2009 — every flow · minute · incomplete · complete · trap

**Status:** RESEARCH LOCK. Do not implement until this year is named as a wave.
**Feel:** one click · Like
**Star:** Facebook Like · `itt09-like` · `sites/facebook/index.html`
**ILS June:** 238,027,855 · 1,766,206,240
**Leftover-official dests in this file:** 264
**Official 10:** frozen in `js/config/flow-trails.js`. **Guided 6:** frozen in `ui/year/start-data.js`.
**Law:** leftover never writes `itt09-like`. Trap / empty / 0 ticks / wrong pick / skip-wait never write. Neighbor-year keys stay empty.
**5k:** research envelope (ILS + Hosting.com + Pew + this dest list). Not 5,000 new rooms.

Open dests as `/years/2009/<href>`. Serve `python3 -m http.server 8080 --bind 127.0.0.1`.

---

## G0 · Guided 6 (frozen · not a 7th item)

Start at `years/2009/pages/home.html` (or the year shell Starting Point).
The painted `<ol>` is **exactly 6**. A 7th `<li>` is a fail.
1. About 2009 — dual scale · bans
2. Facebook Like — two partner pages
3. FarmVille — plant / harvest
4. Bing — decision engine
5. iPhone 3GS — no iPad
6. Year flow map

Walk: About → ★ gold → official leftovers named in the list → map. Visiting a guided dest is not a write. Only the dest machine writes.

---

## G1 · ★ GOLD · `itt09-like`

**Room:** `years/2009/sites/facebook/index.html`
**Hook:** `data-lk09-like`
**Nostalgia:** Facebook Like — this is the year chip. One star.

**Incomplete never writes**
- 0 likes · FarmVille-as-gold trap never write.
- Leftover panel on this room (if any) writes a leftover key, **not** `itt09-like`.

**Complete writes**
- Like the partner pages. Writes itt09-like.
- Payload is REAL (`real: true`) · year `2009` · key `itt09-like`.
- Reload still shows saved. Neighbor `itt-like` — do not write `itt08-*` or `itt10-*` gold.
- **Next after gold:** FarmVille → `years/2009/sites/farmville/index.html`

**Do not:** second star · leftover save that stamps this key · dest-field plaque as the gold machine.

### L-0001 · `itt09-c29` · sites/among/index.html · **ANACHRONISM — do not promote as this year’s memory**

**Open:** `/years/2009/sites/among/index.html`
**Panel:** `[data-lo-panel]:has([data-lo-save][data-lo-key="c29"])`
**Need:** minPick=2
**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `google` · `youtube` · `facebook`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="c29"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt09-c29']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
5. Ticks + fewer than 2 picks + Save → never writes.
7. `itt09-like` stays empty. `itt08-c29` and `itt10-c29` stay empty.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Click 2 distinct `[data-lo-pick]` rows.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="c29"]`.
- `localStorage['itt09-c29']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"2009", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt09-c29`.
- Does **not** write `itt09-like`.
- **Next:** Next leftover → `/years/2009/sites/amz/index.html`
